For multiples of a fixed polynomial, we adapt a comparison argument of Altman and Sawhney to obtain uniformity over the polynomial families produced by the $W$-trick. For distinct degree progressions, we establish a comparison between prime-weighted and unweighted polynomial counts that is uniform throughout the density increment argument and accounts for a possible Siegel zero.

Abstract: The notion of $p$-derivation as introduced by Buium has a rich history of applications in arithmetic geometry. Working over $\ZZ_p$, Buium-Ralph-Simanca showed that arithmetic differential operators built from these determine $p$-adic analytic functions and vice versa. We introduce a notion of analytic functions which are naturally determined by these operators in a way analogous to that in the unramified case, but with a significantly richer structure. Conclusion: 不变性选择机制成功地解释了反射壁数据受阻的本质原因，并确立了四元数代数判别式6、10、22作为特殊模空间（亏格零紧Shimura曲线）的算术意义。权1/2典范形式的存在唯一性与障碍空间的消失完全对应，而截面层上Petersson几何的刚性结构揭示了深刻的超越数联系，这些结果为Borcherds乘积理论、Maass形式和L-函数之间建立了精确的桥梁。

Abstract: The four orders of the quaternion algebra B6 carry four reflective wall data on lattices of signature (3,2); three integrate to Borcherds denominators and one is obstructed. The failed denominator survives as a weakly harmonic Maass form, and we prove its shadow is a Hecke eigenform on the line of the newform 6.4.a.a, with zero twist component. The mechanism is invariance selection: the obstruction functional is invariant under the discriminant isometry group, whose invariants in S_{5/2} are one-dimensional; the same mechanism, verified at quaternion discriminants 10 and 22, places the shadows there on 10.4.a.a and 22.4.a.c. On the weight-1/2 layer we prove a determination theorem: the canonical form exists unconditionally and uniquely precisely when the obstruction space vanishes, and combining the finiteness bound of Bruinier-Ehlen-Freitag with a finite computation this happens exactly for D in {6, 10, 22}, the genus-zero compact Shimura curves, whose maximal-order ternaries are reflective with integral Weyl chambers of ranks 3, 4, 4. Parity confines this layer to odd channels; on the obstructed orientation the section layer is obstructed outside an explicit 40-element locus of orientations, giving a double shadow, CM (36.2.a.a) at weight 3/2 and newform at weight 5/2, while the deck-symmetric directions instead carry a unique canonical weight-1/2 form. The defect invariant satisfies ||Xi||^2 <v_+,v_+> = 144 exactly and equals L(f,2)/(48 pi^2 <f,f>) to 31 digits. On the section layer the Petersson geometry is rigid: the Gram matrix of S_{3/2} is a single transcendental multiple of an exact rational form, and that transcendental is identified, to 40 digits, as 3 Gamma(1/3)^3 / (2^{7/3} pi^2). The weight-3/2 shadow norms lie in the Chowla-Selberg ring from which the weight-5/2 norm is excluded.

Conclusion: 该工作首次为Silverman猜想在几何非分裂半阿贝尔簇上提供了无条件的具体构造，利用了Ribet点的约化性质与等基因核的指数算术，将分母理想的周期性问题归结为有限素位处约化阶的可整除性。

Abstract: Silverman conjectured that the geometric divisibility sequence attached to a Zariski-dense point on an irreducible commutative algebraic group of dimension at least two, with no unipotent part, returns to its initial value infinitely often. We construct, for the firsst time, unconditional examples of this phenomenon on geometrically nonsplit semiabelian varieties over number fields. More precisely, let $A/K$ be a positive-dimensional abelian variety over number field $K$, let $$ 1\longrightarrow\mathbf G_m\xrightarrowιG_q\xrightarrowπA
  \longrightarrow0 $$ be the extension represented by $q\in A^\vee(K)$, and let $R_β(q)\in G_q(K)$ be the normalized Ribet point associated with a homomorphism $β:A^\vee\to A$. Assume that $δ$ is an isogeny and that the cyclic subgroup generated by $δq$ is Zariski dense in $A$. Let $t\in\mathbf G_m(K)$ be a torsion point. Then $G_q$ is geometrically nonsplit and $P=R_β(q)+ι(t)$ has Zariski-dense cyclic orbit. Put $δ=β-\widehatβ$. If $e_δ$ denotes the exponent of $\kerδ$ and $h=\operatorname{ord}(t)$, define $$ N_{δ,t} := \prod_{\ell} \ell^{ \max\left\{ 0, \left\lceil \frac{v_\ell(h)-2v_\ell(e_δ)}{2} \right\rceil \right\}}. $$ If $N_{δ,t}>1$, then $N_{δ,t}\mid d_v(P)$ for all but finitely many finite places $v$, where $d_v(P)$ denotes the order of the reduction of $P$. Consequently, there exists a squarefree integer $Q>1$ such that $$ (n,Q)=1 \quad\Longrightarrow\quad \mathfrak d_{\mathcal N}(nP) =
\mathfrak d_{\mathcal N}(P), $$ where $\mathfrak d_{\mathcal N}$ denotes the full denominator ideal on the Néron lft-model $\mathcal N$.

</details>

Abstract: This paper establishes a new upper bound for the irrationality measure of arctan(1/2), namely 8.585166... Following the strategy and proof techniques of Zudilin and Zeilberger (who improved the bound for pi), we use a family of complex contour integrals with symmetric integrands. The integrality and divisibility of the partial-fraction coefficients are derived via p-adic valuations and a specially defined prime set P_n. Combined with saddle-point asymptotics, the growth rates of the integral sequence and its rational/logarithmic components yield the desired irrationality measure.

</details>

Abstract: Let $F_1=F_2=1$ and $F_{t+2}=F_{t+1}+F_t$ for $t\geq1$. For every $n\geq2$, there are unique integers $a,b,t$ such that $n=aF_t+bF_{t-1}$ with $t\geq2$ and $1\leq a\leq b\leq F_t$. The Fibonacci walk with initial pair $(b,a)$ reaches $n$ as late as possible, and the term following $n$ in this walk is $\lfloorφn\rfloor$ when $t$ is even and $\lceilφn\rceil$ when $t$ is odd, where $φ=(1+\sqrt5)/2$. Let $D=\{d_1<d_2<\cdots\}$ and $U=\{u_1<u_2<\cdots\}$ be the sets corresponding to even and odd $t$, respectively. For $\ell,m\geq1$, define $D_\ell=\{d_{k+\ell}-d_k:k\geq1\}$, $U_\ell=\{u_{k+\ell}-u_k:k\geq1\}$, $D_\ell(m)=\{d_k:d_{k+\ell}-d_k=m\}$ and $U_\ell(m)=\{u_k:u_{k+\ell}-u_k=m\}$. Chung, Graham and Spiro conjectured that $D_\ell=U_\ell$ for all $\ell$, and asked for the densities of $D_\ell(m)$ and $U_\ell(m)$, especially when $\ell=1$. In this paper, we determine the third and fourth order gap spectra, and show that the conjecture holds for $\ell=3$ but fails for $\ell=4$. We also answer their density question by characterizing when $D_\ell(m)$ and $U_\ell(m)$ have natural densities and proving that their logarithmic densities always exist and are equal. For $\ell=1$, we give the exact logarithmic densities.

</details>

Motivation: 数论中关于欧拉函数φ(n)、除数和函数σ(n)和Dedekind函数ψ(n)的性质及其复合函数的研究有着悠久的历史。理解这些复合函数的增长行为及其例外集合的密度是重要问题。此前Sándor以及Dixit和Bhattacharjee等研究者仅获得了密度结果（定性结果），本文旨在提供定量的上界估计，从而更精确地刻画这些例外集合的大小。

Method: 本文采用解析数论方法，利用算术函数的性质以及筛选技巧，对复合函数φ(ψ(n))、φ(σ(n))、ψ(ψ(n))和ψ(σ(n))的例外集合进行定量估计。对于前两个问题，采用可变的非递减阈值函数（满足温和增长条件）替代固定阈值c，从而获得更一般的定量上界；对于后两个问题，建立密度为零的定量估计。

Result: 对每个固定的c>0，获得了满足φ(ψ(n))≥cn和φ(σ(n))≥cn的整数n≤x的数量的定量上界（即密度趋于零的精确速度估计），改进了前人的定性密度结果。同时，建立了ψ(ψ(n))≤cn或ψ(σ(n))≤cn的整数集合的定量密度零估计。此外，将固定阈值推广到满足温和增长条件的非递减函数，使结果更具一般性。

Conclusion: 本文通过获得定量上界，显著改进了关于四类复合算术函数例外集合的已知密度结果。这些定量估计不仅细化了定性结论，而且通过引入更一般的非递减阈值函数，扩展了结果的适用范围，为算术函数复合性质的深入研究提供了更精确的工具。

Abstract: Let \(ψ(n)\), \(φ(n)\), and \(σ(n)\) denote Dedekind's arithmetic function, Euler's totient function, and the sum-of-divisors function, respectively. We study exceptional sets associated with the compositions \(φ(ψ(n))\), \(φ(σ(n))\), \(ψ(ψ(n))\), and \(ψ(σ(n))\). For every fixed \(c>0\), we obtain quantitative upper bounds for the numbers of integers \(n\le x\) satisfying \(φ(ψ(n))\ge cn\) and \(φ(σ(n))\ge cn\), thereby refining density results of Sándor and Dixit and Bhattacharjee, respectively. We further establish quantitative density-zero estimates for the sets of integers \(n\le x\) for which \(ψ(ψ(n))\le cn\) or \(ψ(σ(n))\le cn\). More generally, we allow the fixed thresholds in the first two problems to be replaced by thresholds involving non-decreasing functions subject to mild growth conditions.

*Tapani Matala-aho*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的方法来研究一对实数α和β的联合丢番图逼近问题。通过将联合逼近问题转化为线性丢番图方程xB_m + yB_{m+1} = zD_n + vD_{n+1}的小解问题，利用Thue-Siegel引理保证存在一组有界非零整数解，从而构造公共分母q来同时逼近α和β。对于包括特定等价数在内的一类数，证明了存在κ(α,β)>1/2使得无穷多个明确可构造的整数q满足‖qα‖≤1/q^κ且‖qβ‖≤1/q^κ，这改进了Dirichlet定理并确认了经典Littlewood猜想对该类数对成立。此外还给出了经典Littlewood猜想及其p-adic版本的推广判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Littlewood猜想起源于对实数对同时逼近的研究，断言对于任意实数对(α,β)，总有inf_{q≥1} q·‖qα‖·‖qβ‖ = 0。虽然已知该猜想对其中的某些情况成立，但对一般情况仍为开放问题。作者的动机是通过引入一种全新的"异常方法"（abnormal method），将联合逼近问题转化为一个线性丢番图方程的小解问题，从而在更广泛的条件下验证Littlewood猜想，并独立于传统的Thue-Siegel方法给出显式构造。

Method: 论文的核心方法分为几步：(1) 将联合逼近问题转化为研究线性丢番图方程xB_m + yB_{m+1} = zD_n + vD_{n+1}的小整数解问题，其中B_m和D_n分别是α和β的第m、n个收敛分数的分母。(2) 利用Thue-Siegel引理保证存在非零整数向量(x,y,z,v)使得各分量的绝对值以(B_m+B_{m+1}+D_n+D_{n+1})^{1/3}为上界。(3) 通过该方程构造公共分母q = xB_m + yB_{m+1} = zD_n + vD_{n+1} ≥ 1，再利用连分数理论得到同时逼近。(4) 除了依赖Thue-Siegel引理的构造外，还给出了多种不需要该引理的显式构造方法。(5) 针对特定类别（包括等价数）证明了κ(α,β)>1/2的存在性，并推广到p-adic版本。

Result: 作者成功构造了满足‖qα‖≤1/q^κ和‖qβ‖≤1/q^κ的无穷多个正整数q（κ>1/2），该结果在指数上优于Dirichlet定理（Dirichlet定理给出κ=1/2，且仅保证同时逼近而指数不能同时超越）。该结果同时验证了经典Littlewood猜想对这类数对成立。此外，还给出了经典Littlewood猜想及其p-adic推广的一般性判据。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入将联合逼近问题转化为线性丢番图方程小解研究的"异常方法"，在无须假设Alpha和Beta与有理数接近的前提下，对一类实数对（包括特定等价数）给出了优于Dirichlet定理的显式同时逼近结果，从而验证了Littlewood猜想在该类数对上的成立。此方法的普适性和所给出的显式构造为该二元联立逼近领域提供了新的研究工具，并扩展到了p-adic情形。

Abstract: Let $B_{m}$ and $D_{n}$ be the denominators of the $m$th and $n$th convergent of the real numbers $α$ and $β$, respectively. We introduce an abnormal method in the theory of simultaneous Diophantine approximation to the pair $α,β$. Namely, the question of finding simultaneous approximation is turned into study of small solutions of a linear Diophantine equation $xB_{m} + yB_{m+1} = zD_{n} + vD_{n+1}$. The Thue-Siegel's lemma guarantees the existence of a non-zero integer vector $(x,y,z,v)$ in such a way that $|x|,|y|,|z|,|v|$ are bounded above by $\big( B_{m} + B_{m+1} + D_{n} + D_{n+1} \big)^{1/3}$. Thereby we can construct an integer $q:=xB_{m} + yB_{m+1} = zD_{n} + vD_{n+1}\ge 1$, a common denominator, which by the theory of continued fractions gives simultaneous approximations to $α$ and $β$. We give also a variety of explicit constructions without the Thue-Siegel's lemma. Let $\|α\|:=\underset{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\min\{|α-k|\}$. For a class of numbers, including particular equivalent numbers $α$ and $β$, we show there exist a real number $κ=κ(α,β)>1/2$ and infinitely many explicitly constructible positive integers $q$ such that $\|qα\| \le \frac{1}{q^κ}$ and $\|qβ\| \le \frac{1}{q^κ}$. As the result improves Dirichlet's theorem on simultaneous approximation it also confirms the classical Littlewood conjecture for such a pair $α,β$. In addition, we present general criteria for the classical Littlewood conjecture as well as for its $p$-adic counterpart.

</details>


### [92] [Modularity theorems for Eisenstein congruences in prime-power level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20125)
*Jaclyn Lang,Katharina Müller,Bharathwaj Palvannan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在素数 p 和 N（满足 N≡1 mod p）条件下，权为2的 p-adic Hecke代数在 N 和 N² 两个层级上的 Eisenstein 局部化与某个通用伪形变环的自然商同构，该通用环参数化了在 Np 之外不分歧、在 p 处有限平坦且满足层级相关条件的残差 Eisenstein 伪表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立 Eisenstein 局部化下 Hecke 代数与通用伪形变环之间的同构关系，从而深化对模形式与 Galois 表示之间联系的数学理解，特别是在 Eisenstein 情形下的模性定理。

Method: 论文利用伪表示理论（pseudorepresentation）构造通用伪形变环，并在 N 和 N² 两个层级上分别施加不同的局部条件（在 N 处的条件依赖于层级），通过比较 p-adic Hecke代数的 Eisenstein 局部化与这些通用环的自然商来证明模性定理。

Result: 成功证明了在层级 N 和 N² 下，权为2的 p-adic Hecke代数的 Eisenstein 局部化与通用伪形变环的适当自然商之间存在同构。

Conclusion: 该结果为 Eisenstein 情形下的模性定理提供了新的证明路径，并通过伪形变环的统一框架揭示了不同层级下模性结构的本质联系。

Abstract: Let $p, N \geq 5$ be primes such that $N \equiv 1 \bmod p$. We prove modularity theorems at levels $N$ and $N^2$, showing that suitable Eisenstein localizations of the weight-$2$ $p$-adic Hecke algebra at these levels are isomorphic to certain natural quotients of a universal pseudodeformation ring. This universal ring parametrizes pseudorepresentations that are residually Eisenstein, unramified outside $Np$ and finite-flat at $p$, and satisfy appropriate conditions at $N$ depending on the level.

</details>


### [93] [Variation of Iwasawa Invariants for Ordinary Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20130)
*Abhishek,Chandrakant Aribam,Shiva Barman,Sohan Ghosh*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了数域K上奇素数p的Zp扩张空间中，格林伯格邻域内Selmer群和精细Selmer群的Iwasawa不变量在不同Zp扩张之间的比较结果，并在分圆Zp扩张的邻域内为Selmer群特征理想与Disegni猜想p进L函数之间的联系提供了证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Greenberg在Zp扩张空间中引入了自然拓扑，并建立了经典Iwasawa不变量的有界性结果。本文旨在将这一框架推广到更一般的p进表示（特别是有序p进表示）的Selmer群，比较其在Greenberg邻域内不同Zp扩张上的Iwasawa不变量，并探索与p进L函数猜想的关系。

Method: 作者扩展了Greenberg的拓扑框架，将其应用于附着于有序p进表示的Selmer群。主要方法包括：在Greenberg邻域内系统比较Selmer群和精细Selmer群的Iwasawa不变量（如μ-不变量和λ-不变量）；利用精细Selmer群的结构性质建立类似的有界性结论；在分圆Zp扩张的邻域中，通过分析Selmer群的特征理想，检验其与Disegni提出的猜想p进L函数之间的预期联系。

Result: 本文成功将Greenberg的框架从经典Iwasawa不变量推广到有序p进表示的Selmer群，在Greenberg邻域内建立了Iwasawa不变量的比较与有界性结果，并对精细Selmer群给出了类似结论。此外，在分圆Zp扩张的邻域中，为Selmer群特征理想与Disegni猜想p进L函数之间的联系提供了具体证据。

Conclusion: 作者完成了对有序p进表示Selmer群在Greenberg邻域内Iwasawa不变量的系统比较研究，同时覆盖了精细Selmer群的情形，并初步验证了特征理想与猜想p进L函数之间的深层联系，为后续Iwasawa理论中的p进L函数研究提供了新的证据和方向。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a number field and $p$ be an odd prime. Greenberg introduced a natural topology on the space of all $\mathbb{Z}_p$-extensions of $K$ and established several boundedness results for the classical Iwasawa invariants. We extend this framework to compare the Iwasawa invariants of Selmer groups attached to an ordinary $p$-adic representation across $\mathbb{Z}_p$-extensions lying in a Greenberg neighbourhood in the sense of Greenberg. We also establish analogous results for the fine Selmer groups. Finally, in a neighbourhood of the cyclotomic $\mathbb{Z}_p$-extension, we provide evidence for the expected connection between the characteristic ideal of the Selmer group and the conjectural $p$-adic $L$-function introduced by Disegni.

</details>

Motivation: 线性子空间对具有缓慢Kolmogorov n-宽度衰减的线性控制系统存在局限性，而基于二次流形的模型降阶虽能规避此问题，但文献中缺乏系统的理论框架来构造此类二次近似，即缺少从系统理论角度构建二次投影矩阵的方法。

Method: 提出一个系统无关且免优化的框架，直接构造二次投影矩阵用于降阶模型。该框架在理论上证明了所合成的降阶模型能够匹配全阶系统的非线性矩，并保持精确的中心流形映射，从而在特定输入类别下确保稳态输出的渐近跟踪。

Result: 在传输主导的基准问题（一维阻尼波动方程和对流方程）上，所提出的框架在显著降低的维数状态空间中实现了高保真轨迹重建，带来了实质性的在线计算节省。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了二次流形模型降阶在系统理论框架上的空白，提供了一种无优化开销的直接构造方法，为慢Kolmogorov n-宽度衰减的线性控制系统提供了一条高效、可理论保证的降阶路径。

Abstract: Quadratic manifold-based model order reduction offers a viable pathway to circumvent the limitations of linear subspaces for linear control systems characterized by slow Kolmogorov $n$-width decay. However, a system-theoretic framework for constructing such quadratic approximations remains absent from the literature. This paper presents a system-agnostic, optimization-free framework for the direct construction of quadratic projection matrices. We prove that the synthesized reduced-order model matches the nonlinear moments of the full-order system and preserves its exact center manifold mapping, thereby ensuring asymptotic tracking of steady-state outputs under specific input classes. Numerical results on transport-dominated benchmark problems, namely, the one-dimensional damped wave and advection equations, show that the proposed framework achieves high-fidelity trajectory reconstruction within a significantly reduced-dimensional state space, yielding substantial online computational savings.

</details>


### [95] [On the Value Function of Infinite-Horizon Optimal Control of Piecewise Affine Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19530)
*Francesco Cordiano,Kanghui He,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了分段仿射（PWA）系统在ℓ1或ℓ∞阶段代价约束下的无限时域最优控制（CITOC）问题中值函数的结构，证明值函数可能是非proper PWA（即分段数量可能无穷多），并给出了确保值函数为proper PWA的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究（如文献[1]）仅证明CITOC问题的值函数是状态的分段仿射（PWA）函数，但未分析该值函数是否为proper PWA函数（即在紧集上具有有限个仿射分片），也未回答分片数量是否可能为无穷。这一理论空白限制了值函数性质的深入理解及其在学习和控制方法中的应用。

Method: 作者首先通过一个显式构造的数值示例证明值函数的分片数量确实可以为无穷（即非proper PWA）。基于该示例的启发，建立严谨且易于验证的充分条件，确保CITOC问题中的值函数是proper PWA函数。最后通过数值算例对理论结果进行说明和验证。

Result: 证明了PWA系统CITOC问题的值函数可以是非proper PWA（即具有无穷多个仿射分片）；提出了确保值函数为proper PWA的充分条件；给出的结果补充了线性二次情形等已知结论，并为基于学习的PWA系统控制方案提供了理论支撑。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了PWA系统CITOC值函数结构分析的理论空白，明确了值函数分片有限性的边界条件，为PWA系统的学习型控制与最优控制的交叉研究提供了新的理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the structure of the value function in constrained infinite-time optimal control (CITOC) problems of piecewise affine (PWA) systems, with $\ell_1$ or $\ell_\infty$ stage cost. Existing works, such as [1], establish that the resulting value function is PWA in the state. However, existing results do not analyze whether the value function is a proper PWA function, i.e., with a finite number of affine pieces over compact sets, or whether the number of pieces can be infinite. We show that the latter case is indeed possible by means of an explicit example, which is also instrumental in establishing rigorous and easily verifiable sufficient conditions that ensure that the resulting value function is a proper PWA function. Our theoretical findings complement well-known results, e.g., the linear-quadratic case, and serve as support for recent learning-based control schemes for PWA systems. Throughout the paper, the proposed results are illustrated by means of a numerical example.

</details>


### [96] [Adaptive Time Windows for Discrete Adjoint Topology Optimization of Unsteady Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19617)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需预先设定评估时间间隔，却难以保证所选间隔能够准确代表非定常流动的发展阶段，导致目标评估和伴随分析的准确性受限。

Method: 提出用时间窗序列刻画非定常流动，引入连续时间窗收敛准则自动识别充分发展阶段的代表性时间窗，并在该窗口内进行一致的目标评估与离散伴随分析。实现基于正则化格子Boltzmann方法的大涡模拟（LBM-LES）求解器及部分反弹流固耦合模型。

Result: 连续时间窗收敛准则在后台阶流中验证有效；圆柱绕流应用揭示不同流动状态对框架的影响；尾流恢复优化问题验证了非定常拓扑优化的有效性；U型弯管优化展示了框架识别和重组复杂涡结构的能力。

Conclusion: 该框架无需先验设定评估间隔，可自动识别代表性时间窗，为非定常流动的目标评估、伴随分析和拓扑优化提供了一种一致且有效的通用方法。

Abstract: Rather than prescribing an evaluation interval a priori, the proposed framework characterizes each evolving unsteady flow using a sequence of time windows. A consecutive-window convergence criterion is introduced to automatically identify a representative time window within its fully developed stage. The objective evaluation and discrete adjoint analysis are then carried out consistently over the identified representative time window. The framework is implemented using a regularized lattice Boltzmann method-based large-eddy simulation (LBM-LES) solver together with a partial bounce-back fluid-solid model. The consecutive-window convergence criterion is first validated using the backward-facing step flow. Cylinder-flow applications are then employed to investigate the influence of different flow regimes on the proposed framework. The wake-flow recovery problem verifies its effectiveness for unsteady topology optimization, while U-bend optimization further demonstrates its capability to identify and reorganize complex vortical structures.

</details>


### [97] [High-Multiplicity Flexible Job Shops: From Exact Recurrent Fluid Attainment to Structure-Guided Finite-Horizon Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19711)
*Wenjun Zheng,Wei Qu,Weilin Cai,Jianfeng Mao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究高多重性柔性作业车间调度问题，证明了流体松弛的最优分配经过有限缩放后可被精确实现为可行的重复离散调度，并提出了类型循环模板重放（TCTR）算法。在676个实例上，TCTR均可行，平均与流体下界差距仅1.54%，远优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高多重性柔性作业车间中存在大量相同作业类型的副本，需在多台可选机器上调度。流体松弛虽然能提供可扩展的工作量下界，但其分数形式的机器分配无法直接转化为针对单个作业的可行调度，需要弥合流体松弛与可行离散调度之间的鸿沟。

Method: 首先证明：对流体最优分配进行合适的有限缩放后，可精确构造一个可行的重复离散调度。构造步骤包括：将流体分配缩放到整数操作数、在非重叠的机器区间内放置操作、重复该排列、并通过跨重复链接操作形成单个作业以保持机器可行性。基于此结构，提出类型循环模板重放（TCTR）算法：用不随作业副本数增长的优化模型搜索作业类型模板，并重放所选模板到完整实例。

Result: 在676个多重性扩展的公共柔性作业车间实例上，TCTR在所有情况下均可行，平均与流体下界的差距为1.54%，而作业索引的自适应大邻域搜索为5.32%，混合遗传算法为6.18%。理论上证明了即使有固定数量的额外作业，最优makespan与流体下界之间的差距仍保持有界常数，相对差距随实例规模增长而消失。

Conclusion: 流体松弛经过有限缩放后可以被精确实现为可行的重复离散调度，理论上有界差距，实践上TCTR算法在可行性和调度质量上均显著优于现有方法。该重复结构为高多重性柔性作业车间调度提供了高效且可扩展的求解途径。

Abstract: High-multiplicity flexible job shops involve many copies of a small set of job types that must be scheduled on alternative machines. Fluid relaxations provide scalable workload lower bounds, but their fractional machine allocations do not define feasible schedules for individual jobs. We show that, after a suitable finite scaling, an optimal fluid allocation can be realized exactly by a feasible repeating discrete schedule. The construction scales the fluid allocation to integer operation counts, places the resulting operations in nonoverlapping machine intervals, repeats this arrangement, and links operations across repetitions into individual jobs without moving any interval, thereby enforcing job precedence while preserving machine feasibility. For growing finite instances with the same job-type composition, even with a fixed number of extra jobs, the gap between the optimal makespan and the fluid lower bound remains bounded by a constant; hence the relative gap vanishes as the instance grows. Guided by this repeated structure, we develop type-based cyclic template replay (TCTR), which searches job-type templates using an optimization model whose size does not grow with the number of job copies and replays the selected template on the full instance. On 676 multiplicity-expanded public flexible-job-shop instances, TCTR is feasible in every case and achieves a 1.54% mean gap to the fluid lower bound, compared with 5.32% for a job-indexed adaptive large-neighborhood search and 6.18% for a hybrid genetic algorithm.

</details>


### [98] [Dangling points in area-minimizing Meissner polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19747)
*Beniamin Bogosel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Meissner多面体（由单位直径极端有限集生成的常宽度体）中"悬挂点"（恰好有两个直径邻点的点）在表面积最小化中的作用，证明删除悬挂点不会增加最小表面积，添加悬挂点不会减少最小表面积，从而将寻找面积最小的Meissner多面体的问题简化为无悬挂点的生成集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Meissner多面体是常宽度体的重要构造，其表面积最小化问题具有几何优化意义。悬挂点是否在表面积最小化中起关键作用尚不明确，澄清这一问题有助于缩小搜索空间，简化优化问题。

Method: 作者从极端有限集出发，分析删除悬挂点后生成集对应的Meissner多面体表面积的变化规律，通过两种互补的证明：删除悬挂点不会使最小表面积增大，以及添加悬挂点不会使最小表面积减小，从两个方向建立结论。

Result: 证明了删除悬挂点后，基于新生成集的Meissner多面体的最小表面积不超过原最小表面积；添加悬挂点后，最小表面积不减少。因此面积最小的Meissner多面体可以由无悬挂点的生成集构造。

Conclusion: 悬挂点在表面积最小化中并非必要，可将搜索范围限定在无悬挂点的极端生成集上，这显著简化了面积最小化Meissner多面体的寻找问题。

Abstract: Meissner polyhedra are constant-width bodies obtained from extremal finite sets of unit diameter. Such a generating set may contain dangling points, namely points having exactly two diametric neighbors. This article studies whether these points can play an essential role in surface-area minimization. Given an extremal set we show that the smallest surface area among the Meissner polyhedra based on it cannot increase by deleting a dangling point. Adding a dangling point cannot decrease the smallest achievable surface area. This reduces the search for area-minimizing Meissner polyhedra to generating sets without dangling points.

</details>


### [99] [A Controllability Gramain Shaping with LMI Constraints under Bures--Wasserstein Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19754)
*Koju Nishimoto,Yuki Onishi,Riku Funada,Mitsuji Sampei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过塑造可控性格拉姆矩阵（controllability Gramian）来设计外生输入对系统状态影响的控制器设计方法。利用Bures-Wasserstein距离将塑形问题建模为系统Gramian与期望Gramian之间距离的最小化，并证明目标函数在对称正定矩阵集合上严格凸。通过LMI推导出半定规划（SDP）公式以提高计算效率并允许加入额外LMI约束。在高斯白噪声输入场景下，该框架与H2控制紧密相关，可视为最优传输的特例。数值实验验证了引导机器人的各向异性可控性设计及方向约束施加能力，并确认当期望Gramian趋近于零时方法趋近H2控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可控性设计通常只关注系统是否可控，缺乏对外生输入影响系统状态的具体方向或幅值进行设计的工具。作者希望开发一种系统化的方法，使可控性格拉姆矩阵能够被塑造成期望的形式，从而精确设计外生输入对状态的影响，同时保持计算上的可解性。

Method: 1) 采用Bures-Wasserstein距离作为系统Gramian与期望Gramian之间的度量，建立最小化问题的目标函数；2) 证明目标函数在对称正定矩阵集合上的严格凸性；3) 将凸优化问题转化为基于LMI的半定规划（SDP）公式，提升计算效率并支持附加约束；4) 揭示该框架在高斯白噪声输入下与H2控制及最优传输之间的联系。

Result: 数值实验表明：（1）所提方法能够对引导机器人实现各向异性可控性设计；（2）能够通过LMI施加额外的方向性约束；（3）当期望Gramian趋于零时，所提方法收敛到H2控制的结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立的Gramian塑形框架为控制器设计提供了新的工具，将可控性设计与凸优化、最优传输和H2控制统一起来，兼具理论优雅性与实际可计算性，为各向异性输入影响设计提供了有效途径。

Abstract: This paper proposes a controller design method for shaping the controllability Gramian into a desired form to design the effect from exogenous inputs to the system state. Using the Bures--Wasserstein distance, we formulate the shaping problem as the minimization of the distance between the system Gramian and a desired Gramian, and the objective function is shown to be strictly convex on the set of symmetric positive definite matrices. In addition, by deriving a semidefinite programming formulation via a linear matrix inequality (LMI), computational efficiency is improved and additional LMI constraints can be incorporated. When the exogenous input is modeled as Gaussian white noise, the proposed framework is closely related to $H_2$ control, which can be interpreted as a special case of optimal transport. Numerical examples demonstrate anisotropic controllability design for a guidance robot and verify the ability to impose additional directional constraints through LMIs. The numerical examples also confirm that the proposed method approaches $H_2$ control as the desired Gramian tends to zero.

</details>


### [100] [A Fixed-Penalty Linearized Augmented Lagrangian Method with Classical Multiplier Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19847)
*Benqi Liu,Kangkang Deng,Zichen Wang,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种非线性残差线性化增广拉格朗日方法（NR-LALM），用于平滑非凸的确定性或随机等式约束优化问题。该方法用正则化高斯-牛顿步替代昂贵的非线性原始子问题求解，同时保留经典基于非线性约束残差的乘子更新，从单个对称正定线性系统计算步长。在局部正则性下可控制约束线性化误差，确定性情形在O(ε⁻²)次迭代内找到ε-近似KKT对，随机情形需O(ε⁻³)次随机梯度评估。所有理论结果在Lean 4中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增广拉格朗日方法对非线性等式约束优化有效，但其非线性原始子问题的求解计算成本高昂。对于平滑非凸问题（无论是确定性还是随机目标），需要一种能避免求解昂贵非线性子问题同时保留经典乘子更新优势的方法，以提高计算效率。

Method: 提出的NR-LALM方法将增广拉格朗日子问题替换为正则化高斯-牛顿型步，基于非线性约束残差进行经典乘子更新。步长由单一对称正定线性系统计算。对于随机目标，使用带保护重启的投影随机路径积分微分估计器。还引入可选的最小范数二阶校正项，将约束线性化误差从二阶降低到四阶

Result: 确定性NR-LALM在O(ε⁻²)次迭代和一阶预言评估内找到ε-近似KKT对；随机目标在期望O(ε⁻³)次随机梯度评估和O(ε⁻²)次约束及Jacobian评估内收敛。紧性与Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz条件保证确定性原始-对偶序列的有限长度收敛。数值实验确认了预测的误差阶数，并在高维确定性和随机问题上表现良好。

Conclusion: NR-LALM通过线性化原始模型解决增广拉格朗日子问题的高成本问题，同时保留经典乘子更新的非线性残差信息。所有理论结果在Lean 4中形式化证明，确保数学严谨性。该方法在确定性和随机非凸等式约束优化中均实现了理论保证的复杂度和良好的实际性能。

Abstract: Augmented Lagrangian methods are effective for nonlinear equality-constrained optimization, but solving their nonlinear primal subproblems can be expensive. For smooth nonconvex problems with deterministic or stochastic objectives, we propose a nonlinear-residual linearized augmented Lagrangian method (NR-LALM) that replaces this subproblem by a regularized Gauss-Newton-type step while retaining the classical multiplier update based on the nonlinear constraint residual. The resulting step is computed from one symmetric positive-definite linear system, but the mismatch between the linearized primal model and the nonlinear-residual update produces a quadratic constraint-linearization error in the multiplier identity. We show that this error can be controlled under local regularity; multiplier boundedness and trajectory localization are derived rather than assumed. With fixed, accuracy-independent parameters, deterministic NR-LALM finds an $\varepsilon$-approximate Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) pair in $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ iterations and first-order oracle evaluations. For stochastic objectives, a projected stochastic path-integrated differential estimator with safeguarded restarts requires, in expectation, $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ stochastic-gradient evaluations and $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ constraint and Jacobian evaluations. Compactness and a Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz condition further yield finite-length convergence of the deterministic primal-dual sequence. An optional minimum-norm second-order correction reduces the constraint-linearization error from second to fourth order without changing the complexity orders. All theoretical results are formalized in Lean 4. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted error orders and show favorable performance on high-dimensional deterministic and stochastic problems.

</details>


### [101] [To Control or not to Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19862)
*Odysseas Kanavetas,Camiel M. P. Koopmans,Floske M. Spieksma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文引入带控制休假（control vacations）的M/M/1排队模型，区别于传统的永久控制队列，在控制期结束后进入指数分布的休假期并使用固定服务率μ，后续控制期的启动需花费成本调度。作者基于Kanavetas等人的MDP框架，给出了确保平均期望成本低于仅用固定服务率模型的充分条件，并利用相关过程性质构造成本降低策略。通过更新奖励过程和Tauberian定理，将平均期望成本与消失折扣方法关联，解析出平均期望成本降低的下界，最后通过仿真验证实际成本降低效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统排队控制系统中控制机制是永久有效的，但在实际场景中，持续控制可能带来昂贵的调度或监控成本。因此需要研究临时性控制（即控制间插入休假）的模型，以在控制成本和系统性能之间取得更好的平衡。

Method: 1) 基于Kanavetas等人的马尔可夫决策过程框架建立M/M/1队列模型，其中控制期长度为指数分布，之后进入控制休假期采用固定服务率μ。2) 推导一个充分条件，保证平均期望成本优于仅使用固定服务率的基准模型。3) 利用相关过程的性质，在满足该条件下构造成本降低策略。4) 特定策略下控制休假过程诱导更新奖励过程，利用Tauberian定理将平均期望成本与消失折扣方法相关联，解析求出平均期望成本降低的下界。5) 通过仿真研究各策略实际达到的平均成本降低幅度。

Result: 给出了控制休假模型平均期望成本低于固定服务率模型的充分条件，构造了成本降低策略，并利用Tauberian定理解析确定了平均期望成本降低下界，最终通过仿真验证了策略的实际成本降低效果。

Conclusion: 控制休假机制可以在特定条件下有效降低排队系统的平均期望成本，为成本敏感型排队控制提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce a model with control vacations instead of standard queueing control systems with permanent control. The researched model is an M/M/1 queue with temporary periods of service rate control with two available service rates. After a control period of exponentially distributed length, a control vacation is initiated during which a fixed service rate $μ$ is used. The start of the next control period needs to be scheduled directly at a certain cost. We will use the Markov Decision Process from Kanavetas et al. arXiv:2605.31573 to find a sufficient condition that ensures that the average expected cost can be reduced w.r.t. the model that only uses the fixed service rate. Under this condition we will use properties of this related process to construct a cost reducing policy. The process with control vacations under specific policies induces a renewal reward process. We use a Tauberian theorem to relate the average expected cost of this renewal reward process to a vanishing discount method and analytically determine a lower bound of the average expected cost reduction. Finally, we study the actual attained average cost reduction for these policies through simulation.

</details>


### [102] [Efficient flatness-based computation of trajectories for vehicles with many trailers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19915)
*Basile Graf,Gustave Lapierre,Philippe Müllhaupt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于微分平坦性的高效馈入转向输入计算方法，用于带n辆拖车的汽车。通过结合四个算法组件（角度中间变量、子映射因子化递归、重标定高阶乘积/商规则、基于截断形式幂级数的复合规则），实现了O(r³)的算术复杂度，对比SymPy符号微分和Bell多项式方法均有数量级提升，并成功用于20辆拖车的泊车演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标准方法——对平坦输出进行迭代符号微分——其表达式规模随拖车数量n呈超指数增长，即使借助计算机代数软件，当n超过约5时也变得难以处理。这严重限制了微分平坦性方法在n拖车系统中的实际应用，亟需一种可扩展的高效计算方法。

Method: 作者提出一个组合算法，利用四个关键组件：(1)角度中间变量以简化表达式；(2)将递归因子化为子映射以减少符号复杂度；(3)重标定的高阶乘积/商规则；以及(4)基于截断形式幂级数的复合规则替代Faa di Bruno公式。该算法在导数阶数r上达到O(r³)的算术复杂度。

Result: 基准测试中，SymPy在导数阶r=14时达到20秒超时，Bell多项式递归在r=24之后超过60秒超时；而所提方法在r=40以内保持亚毫秒级计算时间，在参考方法可行的范围内与数值机器精度一致。端到端示例展示了20拖车泊车机动的高效计算与动画演示。

Conclusion: 该算法显著突破了n拖车系统微分平坦性计算的规模瓶颈，将复杂度从超指数降低到多项式级别，为实际应用（如多拖车自动泊车、重型车辆操纵）提供了实用的计算工具。

Abstract: We address the practical computation of feedforward steering inputs for a car with n trailers via differential flatness. The standard route (iterated symbolic differentiation of the flat output) scales prohibitively with n: even with computer-algebra software the expression sizes grow super-exponentially and become intractable beyond n approx. 5. We propose an algorithm that combines four ingredients (angular intermediate variables, factorisation of the recursion in submaps, rescaled higher-order product/quotient rules, and a composition rule based on truncated formal power series instead of Faa di Bruno's formula). We benchmark the proposed method against (i) direct symbolic differentiation in SymPy and (ii) Faa di Bruno's formula evaluated via Bell polynomials. Both reference methods hit a clear computational wall: SymPy reaches a 20 s timeout at derivative order r = 14, while the Bell-polynomial recursion exceeds a 60 s timeout beyond r = 24. The proposed approach has O(r3) arithmetic complexity in the derivative order and remains under one millisecond up to r = 40, with numerical agreement to machine precision in the regime where the reference methods succeed. As an end-to-end illustration, a 20-trailer parking manoeuvre is computed and animated.

</details>


### [103] [Dual Control: On Exploration-Exploitation in Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20073)
*Tomas J. Meijer,Anders Rantzer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文综述了线性时不变系统中参数未知时的"对偶控制"理论，涵盖四大研究方向：多臂老虎机、自整定调节器、最小遗憾率控制器和极大极小最优对偶控制器，其中后者为鲁棒对偶控制提供了有前景的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对偶控制问题（同时平衡探索与利用）已有近百年研究历史，但缺乏对该领域主要研究方向的系统性梳理。本文旨在为参数未知且需主动探测的线性时不变系统的最优控制提供理论和方法论综述。

Method: 本文采用文献综述的方法，系统梳理和比较了四个主要研究方向：多臂老虎机、自整定调节器、最小遗憾率控制器以及极大极小最优对偶控制器，对各方向的核心思想进行了归纳与评述。

Result: 前三个研究方向（多臂老虎机、自整定调节器、最小遗憾率控制器）已有悠久历史和丰富文献，而极大极小最优对偶控制器作为一个新兴方向，为鲁棒对偶控制提供了有前景的理论框架。

Conclusion: 本文明确指出了对偶控制四个主要研究方向的发展现状，特别强调了极大极小最优对偶控制作为未来鲁棒对偶控制框架的潜力。

Abstract: The term "dual control" refers to the dual objective of simultaneously balancing exploration and exploitation. Problems of this kind have been studied for nearly a century. This paper is devoted to theory and methodology relevant for optimal control of linear time-invariant systems whose parameters are initially unknown and must be learned by active probing. We review the main ideas underlying four major research directions: Multi-armed bandits, self-tuning regulators, regret rate minimizing controllers, and minimax optimal dual controllers. The first three have a long history and rich literature, whereas the fourth provides a promising framework for robust dual control.

</details>


### [104] [Nonlinear Controllability and the Propagation of Local Information: From the Kalman Family to Lie Brackets, Rotation Groups, and Reachable Subgroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20094)
*Philippe Mullhaupt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文发展了一条从线性可控性到矩阵李群上非线性可控性的自包含李理论路径，通过李括号、单参数子群和Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff公式将局部代数信息传播为可达集陈述，并重点分析了旋转群SO(n)上的可控性几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性可控性理论中，Cayley-Hamilton定理和Kalman族B,AB,...,A^{n-1}B可将可控性归结为有限维线性代数问题；但在非线性矩阵李群情形下，李代数可能无穷维，有限维李对应关系可能失效。作者旨在建立一条系统的李理论路线，回答"局部代数信息能否传播为可达集的全局陈述"这一基本问题。

Method: 本文采用系统化的李理论方法：(1) 以线性情形为模型，利用矩阵指数、Cayley-Hamilton定理、轨迹公式和Kalman族建立基础；(2) 在非线性情形中以李括号代替矩阵幂，借助斜对称矩阵的指数、交换子、单参数子群和BCH公式研究SO(n)上的无穷小方向传播；(3) 通过可达到子群论证处理右不变系统，给出Yamabe步骤的详细证明序列；(4) 用SO(3)数值仿真及SO(4)和SO(7)交换子序列展示控制方向的传播；(5) 通过上三角矩阵代数的可解、幂零和理想实例说明有限维机制，并与Sussmann的通用非线性系统局部可控性理论进行对比。

Result: 本文成功建立了从线性到非线性可控性的李理论统一框架。SO(3)仿真揭示了受控旋转的几何结构，SO(4)和SO(7)的交换子序列使局部控制方向的传播可视化。可达到子群论证和Yamabe步骤的完整证明为右不变系统提供了严格的数学基础。上三角矩阵代数的可解、幂零和理想实例阐明了有限维机制与Sussmann理论的联系与区别。

Conclusion: 本文表明，在李群框架下，局部的无穷小信息（李代数中的控制方向）可以通过李括号传播、单参数子群生成和交换子序列等机制，系统地推广为可达集的全局可控性结论。这一路线特别适用于旋转群SO(n)，并为进一步理解非线性可控性与有限维代数结构之间的关系提供了统一的李理论视角。

Abstract: This article develops a self-contained Lie-theoretic route from linear controllability to nonlinear controllability on matrix Lie groups. The organizing question is whether local algebraic information can be propagated into statements about reachable sets. The linear case supplies the model: the matrix exponential, Cayley-Hamilton theorem, trajectory formula and Kalman family B,AB,...,An-1B reduce controllability to finite-dimensional linear algebra. In the nonlinear setting, Lie brackets replace matrix powers, but the associated Lie algebra may be infinite dimensional and the finite-dimensional Lie correspondence can fail. Particular emphasis is placed on rotation groups. Skew-symmetric matrices, their exponentials, commutators, one-parameter subgroups, and the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff formula provide a concrete laboratory for seeing how infinitesimal directions propagate on SO(n). A numerical SO(3) simulation illustrates the geometry of controlled rotations, while explicit SO(4) and SO(7) commutator sequences make the propagation of a localized control direction visible. The article then develops the attainable-subgroup argument for right-invariant systems, gives a detailed proof sequence behind the Yamabe step, and works through solvable, nilpotent and ideal examples for upper-triangular matrix algebras. The finite-dimensional mechanism is finally contrasted with Sussmann's local controllability theory for general nonlinear systems.

</details>


### [105] [Worst-Case Probability Bounds for Finite-Horizon Safety under Moment Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20121)
*Renato Loureiro,Torbjørn Cunis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用矩-平方和（moment-SOS）框架来估计动力系统在有限时间范围内进入不良区域的概率上界的方法。该方法将问题建模为基于测度的规划，并通过其对偶SOS规划实现求解，得到的概率性证书在结构上与经典障碍函数方法相似。通过多个案例（包括轨道物体）验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力系统在有限时间内进入不安全区域（不良区域）的概率估计是安全性验证中的核心问题。实际中系统的初始状态通常无法精确已知，仅能获取其有限阶矩或给定区间信息。现有确定性安全验证方法（如障碍函数）无法处理概率性质的安全性保证，因此需要一种能够利用初始状态部分信息（矩信息）来估计进入不良区域概率上界的方法。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为一个基于测度的优化问题，利用矩-平方和（moment-SOS）框架对其进行松弛求解。具体地，构造了原问题的对偶问题——一个函数规划，并将其强化为平方和（SOS）规划。该SOS规划的解可以解释为一种概率性的障碍函数证书，为系统在有限时域内进入不良区域的概率提供上界。

Result: 所提出的方法通过多个案例研究验证了其有效性，包括一个轨道物体运动的场景。结果表明该方法能够基于有限的初始状态矩信息，提供关于系统进入不良区域概率的上界估计。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将安全验证中的障碍函数思想推广到概率场景，通过moment-SOS框架实现了仅依赖初始状态矩信息即可估计有限时间内系统进入不良区域的概率上界，为不确定动力系统的概率安全性分析提供了新的理论工具和计算方法。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of estimating upper bounds on the probability that a dynamical system will enter an undesirable region at some point within a finite time horizon. The primary source of uncertainty lies in the system's initial state, for which only a finite set of moments is known or within a prescribed interval. To tackle this problem, we formulate a measure-based program and propose its relaxation using the moment-sum-of-squares (moment-SOS) framework. The corresponding dual problem is introduced as a functional program, which is subsequently strengthened into a sum-of-squares (SOS) program. Notably, this dual formulation bears a structural resemblance to classical barrier function techniques for certifying system safety, with the key distinction that it yields a probabilistic certificate. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is demonstrated through multiple case studies, including a case involving an object in orbit.

</details>


### [106] [Dynamic Portfolio Optimization under CVaR Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20179)
*Anran Hu,Silvana M. Pesenti,Xiaofei Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间动态投资组合优化中终端损失的条件风险价值（CVaR）约束问题。利用CVaR的辅助阈值表示，对一般凸交易目标在不需要市场完备性的条件下建立了最优策略存在性与强对偶性，并提出基于对偶的双层嵌套二分-黄金分割搜索算法。数值实验验证了约束非绑定时的Merton策略以及约束绑定时的状态依赖型策略特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态投资组合优化中引入终端CVaR约束具有重要的风险管理意义，但现有研究大多依赖于市场完备性假设，且缺乏对一般凸目标函数下最优策略存在性及高效数值求解算法的系统性理论保证。

Method: 利用CVaR的辅助阈值表示将约束问题转化为可对偶化形式；在一般凸交易目标下免除市场完备性条件，建立最优策略存在性与强对偶性；提出基于阈值与Lagrange乘子的双层嵌套二分-黄金分割搜索算法，内层迭代归约为标准无约束随机控制问题；证明迭代次数趋于无穷时策略收敛到最优控制。

Result: 数值实验显示：约束非绑定时恢复Merton策略；约束绑定时最优策略呈状态依赖——不利结果后投资者降低风险敞口，有利结果后保持甚至临近到期时增加敞口，表明终端CVaR约束产生跨状态的非对称再配置而非统一降风险。此外，非交易禀赋风险放大保守调整幅度，价格冲击降低期望头寸与调整速度。

Conclusion: 终端CVaR约束在动态组合优化中导致非对称的状态依赖型风险再配置；所提对偶算法在一般凸目标及非完备市场下具有理论收敛保证，揭示了禀赋风险与价格冲击对风险对冲行为的不同影响机制。

Abstract: We study continuous-time dynamic portfolio optimization under a Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) constraint on the investor's terminal loss. For a general class of convex trading objectives, we exploit the auxiliary-threshold representation of CVaR to establish the existence of an optimal strategy and strong duality without requiring market completeness. These results motivate a dual-based nested bisection--golden-search algorithm over the threshold and Lagrangian multiplier, where the inner iterations reduce to standard unconstrained stochastic control problems. We prove that the resulting strategies converge to the optimal control as the number of iterations tends to infinity. Numerical experiments recover the Merton policy when the risk constraint is nonbinding. When the constraint is binding, the optimal strategy becomes state dependent: the investor reduces risky exposure following adverse outcomes but preserves, and near maturity may increase, exposure following favorable outcomes. Thus, a terminal CVaR constraint produces an asymmetric reallocation across states rather than uniform de-risking. Nontraded endowment risk amplifies the conservative adjustment, whereas price impact lowers desired positions and adjustment speeds.

</details>


### [107] [Direct vs. Indirect Data-Driven Control: Case-study of Switching Systems Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20207)
*Alexis Vuille,Guillaume O. Berger,Raphaël M. Jungers*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于间接方法（先辨识系统模型，再应用基于模型的控制技术）的切换线性系统稳定性分析框架，利用二次Lyapunov分析处理带噪声的状态测量数据，并提供有限样本收敛率保证。通过结合机器学习的泛化界、系统辨识和Lyapunov分析的灵敏度分析实现。实验表明在中高噪声水平下，间接方法比直接方法具有更紧的概率保证和更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动控制中，直接方法（直接从数据推断控制器或证书）近期已在切换线性系统的有限样本稳定性保证方面取得进展，但间接方法（先辨识模型再应用模型基技术）的有限样本保证仍鲜有涉及，形成了方法论上的空白。

Method: 1）采用间接方法，对含噪声状态测量的切换线性系统进行稳定性分析；2）结合机器学习的泛化界、系统辨识理论与二次Lyapunov分析的灵敏度分析，推导有限样本收敛率保证；3）将现有的直接数据驱动方法扩展至超越有界噪声的更一般噪声情形，以便公平对比。

Result: 通过数值实验对比两种方法，发现在中等到高噪声水平下，间接方法能提供更紧的概率保证，且对噪声和离群值具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 间接方法在含噪环境下相较于直接方法具有理论保证上的优势和更强的实用鲁棒性，为数据驱动稳定性分析提供了更可靠的选择。

Abstract: A central methodological question in data-driven control is whether to adopt a direct or indirect approach. Direct methods infer a controller or certificate directly from data, while indirect methods first identify a system model and then apply model-based control techniques. Recent developments of the direct method have led to finite-sample guarantees for the data-driven stability analysis of switched linear systems under various settings. However, for the indirect method, such guarantees remain largely elusive. In this paper, we provide a novel framework for the stability analysis of switched linear systems from noisy state measurements, using the indirect approach and quadratic Lyapunov analysis. Our framework comes with finite-sample guarantees on the convergence rate of the system. For that, we combine generalization bounds from machine learning and system identification with sensitivity analysis from quadratic Lyapunov analysis. To enable comparison, we also extend existing direct data-driven methods to handle measurement noise beyond the bounded noise case currently available in the literature. Finally, we compare the two approaches through numerical experiments, revealing that under moderate-to-high noise levels the indirect approach yields tighter probabilistic guarantees as well as greater robustness to noise and outliers than the direct approach

</details>


### [108] [Directional Subdifferentials of the Value Function in Asplund Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20241)
*Weihao Mao,Jane J. Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在Asplund空间中建立了值函数的方向次微分变分框架，引入新的方向性条件以克服无穷维空间中有界集缺乏紧性的困难，推导出值函数的方向极限次微分和奇异次微分的上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有值函数方向次微分的结果主要局限于有限维空间，而实际应用中（如无穷维分层系统）需要无穷维框架下的方向敏感性分析。

Method: 在Asplund空间中建立方向性变分框架，通过引入新的方向性条件来处理无穷维空间中有界集非紧的问题，进而建立方向次微分演算规则。

Result: 获得了值函数方向极限次微分和方向奇异次微分的上界估计，扩展了方向非光滑分析到无穷维设定。

Conclusion: 该框架为无穷维分层系统的灵敏度分析提供了更精细的分析基础。

Abstract: Directional subdifferentials of the value function provide a quantitative measure of optimal value response to perturbations. While existing results are largely limited to finite-dimensional settings, this paper develops a comprehensive variational framework in Asplund spaces. We establish essential directional calculus rules, extending directional nonsmooth analysis to infinite dimensions. To address the lack of compactness of bounded sets in infinite-dimensional spaces, we introduce a new directional condition, under which we derive upper estimates for directional limiting and singular subdifferentials of the value function. These results provide a refined analytical foundation for sensitivity analysis in infinite-dimensional hierarchical systems.

</details>

### [109] [A stochastic dose-response framework for environmentally persistent pathogens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19607)
*Mahmudul Bari Hridoy,Arik Hartmann,Kate E. Langwig,Joseph R. Hoyt,Lauren M. Childs*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个随机剂量-反应框架，将宿主感染动态与环境病原体储库结合，用于研究环境传播疾病的病原入侵与持久性。该框架采用连续时间马尔可夫链和分支过程近似估计灭绝概率，并纳入季节性因素，以蛇真菌病为案例进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境持久性病原体通过直接接触和环境污染间接暴露两种途径传播，其入侵与持久性受到剂量-反应关系、随机性和季节性变化的共同影响。理解这些因素如何塑造病原体的入侵与存续是环境传播疾病系统的重要挑战。

Method: 构建随机剂量-反应框架，整合宿主感染动态与明确的环境病原体储库；采用随机连续时间马尔可夫链公式化模型，利用分支过程近似估计感染宿主或环境病原体载量低时的疾病灭绝概率；扩展框架纳入宿主易感性、环境接触和宿主-宿主接触的季节性变化；以蛇真菌病为案例进行数值模拟和全局敏感性分析。

Result: 数值模拟显示剂量-反应影响流行病起飞和感染水平；季节性创造了高与低灭绝风险的窗口；灭绝风险强烈依赖于引入的途径和季节性周期内的引入时机。全局敏感性分析进一步揭示了随机性、非线性剂量-反应和季节性时机对疫情动态的影响。

Conclusion: 随机性、非线性剂量-反应关系和季节性时机共同塑造环境持久性病原体的爆发动态，该框架为理解环境传播疾病的入侵与持久性提供了系统性的理论工具。

Abstract: Infectious diseases caused by environmentally persistent pathogens can strongly affect host populations as transmission occurs not only through direct host-host contact but also via indirect exposure to contaminated environments. While in some systems environmental reservoirs help sustain exposure even when infected host numbers are low, reliance on environmental transmission pathway may also increase pathogen extinction risk. Infection risk may depend on both pathogen dose and timing of exposure. Thus, understanding how dose-response, stochasticity, and seasonal changes in host susceptibility or contact rates shape pathogen invasion and persistence remains an important challenge for environmentally transmitted disease systems. To address this, we develop a stochastic dose-response framework that integrates host infection dynamics with an explicit environmental pathogen reservoir. Transmission occurs through both direct contact with infectious hosts and indirect environmental exposure, with infection probability governed by dose-response functions. We focus on stochastic continuous-time Markov chain formulation and use branching process approximation to estimate disease extinction probabilities when infected hosts or environmental pathogen loads are low. We extend the framework to include seasonality in host susceptibility, environmental contact, and host-host contact. As a case study, we apply the model to snake fungal disease. Numerical simulations show that dose-response influences epidemic takeoff and infection levels, while seasonality creates windows of high and low extinction risk. These extinction risks depend strongly on the route and timing of introduction within the seasonal cycle. These results, coupled with global sensitivity analysis, illustrate how stochasticity, nonlinear dose-response, and seasonal timing shape outbreak dynamics for environmentally persistent pathogens.

</details>


### [110] [Correlations at criticality in ecological communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20086)
*Akiva Goldberg,Nadav M. Shnerb*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文证明生态群落即使在任意接近临界稳定性的情况下，特征值软模也不一定在随机环境强迫下主导涨落，因此近临界性未必体现在等时两两相关性中，单模图像可能失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态群落不断被入侵、排斥和多样化重塑，自然地被推向动力稳定性的边界。近边界处软模弛豫缓慢，理论预测其应在随机强迫下主导涨落，将动力学降为一维并产生强正负丰度相关性，此类相关性被视为即将发生转变的签名。作者希望检验这一普遍预期是否总是成立。

Method: 作者通过理论分析与数值模拟，在三种生态场景中检验软模主导涨落的预测：合成的可行群落、由区域库迁入形成的局地群落、以及反复多样化生成的群落。关键是考察谱软性（spectral softness）是否足以保证随机可见性（stochastic visibility），即软模须获得足够的环境强迫才能在其余模态产生的涨落背景中占据主导。

Result: 在全部三种场景中，群落都趋向边际稳定性，但并未发展出单模图像所预测的近完美两两相关性。证明谱软性并不保证随机可见性：即使任意接近临界性，软模也可能因环境强迫不足而无法在涨落中占主导。

Conclusion: 接近生态临界性并不必然在等时两两相关性中可见，以相关性作为临界转变预警信号需谨慎，单模降维描述可能在某些条件下失效。

Abstract: Ecological communities are continually reshaped by invasion, exclusion, and diversification, processes that naturally drive them toward the boundary of dynamical stability. Near such a boundary, a soft mode relaxes increasingly slowly and, under stochastic forcing, is expected to dominate the fluctuations, effectively reducing the dynamics to one dimension and generating strong positive and negative abundance correlations. Such correlations have therefore been proposed as signatures of an imminent transition. Here we show that this expectation can fail even arbitrarily close to criticality. The reason is that spectral softness does not guarantee stochastic visibility: the soft mode must receive enough environmental forcing to dominate the fluctuation background generated by the remaining modes. We demonstrate this mechanism in three ecological scenarios: a synthetic feasible community, a local community assembled by immigration from a regional pool, and a community generated by repeated diversification. In all three, communities approach marginal stability without developing the near-perfect pairwise correlations predicted by the single-mode picture. Thus, proximity to ecological criticality need not be visible in equal-time pairwise correlations.

</details>


### [111] [Novel models of trait evolution via an expansion of Lande's fitness function: The Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process meets the Little Prince's boa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20232)
*Jose M Ponciano,Claire Godineau,Laura Jimenez,Nicholas Kortessis,Rosana Zenil-Ferguson,Robert D Holt*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文重新审视并推广了Lande 1976年的适应性地形（adaptive topography）模型，将其从单一适应度分量扩展到两个适应度分量。研究表明，双分量的最优适应度函数是两个分量最优值的加权平均，权重由各分量的稳定选择强度决定。时空异质性可产生非对称、双峰等新颖的适应度函数形状，并衍生出一类新的随机微分方程（OU模型）族，为非高斯适应度函数提供了机制解释，并为测试替代模型和研究生态与进化动态的相互作用开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lande 1976年的适应性地形理论是进化生物学的基础，但其原始推导仅适用于单一适应度分量。自然系统中的适应度往往由多个分量（如生存与繁殖）共同决定，因此需要将适应性地形推广到多适应度分量的情形，以更准确地描述和理解进化过程。

Method: 作者从数学上推广了Lande的适应性地形推导，将其扩展到两个适应度分量。推导出最优适应度函数为两个分量最优值的加权平均；通过案例研究展示时空异质性如何产生不同的适应度函数形状（非对称、双峰或缺乏双峰）；并利用Lande的方法将新适应度公式与Ornstein-Uhlenbeck模型结合，推导出一族新的随机微分方程模型。

Result: 推导出双适应度分量的适应性地形：最优值为分量最优的加权平均。发现时空异质性可产生非对称、双峰等新颖的适应度函数形态。结合OU模型框架，衍生出新的随机微分方程模型族，为非高斯适应度函数提供了机制性解释。

Conclusion: 该工作为自然系统中常见的非高斯适应度函数提供了机制性依据，提供了检验不同非高斯适应度函数生成模型的路径，并为研究生态与进化动态的相互作用（如进化救援）奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Adaptive topographies form the foundation for much of our understanding of evolutionary change. Lande's 1976 influential paper on the adaptive topography of phenotypes demonstrated how the concept is inherent in both phenotypic and genetic models of evolution, and how the concept can be used to test evolutionary hypotheses given data. Here, we revisit and generalize Lande's original derivation of an equation analogous to Wright's genotypic adaptive topography to the case of two fitness components. A move to two fitness components yields novel predictions about the shape and mechanistic underpinnings of the adaptive topography. The optimum of this updated fitness function is a weighted average of the optima of the two fitness components, with weights given by the relative strengths of stabilizing selection on each component. Temporal or spatial heterogeneity in the strengths of selection for each fitness component create novel shapes (asymmetry, bi-modality, or lack thereof) of the overall fitness function, a possibility demonstrated with a case-study from the published literature. Finally, when combined with Lande's approach to generate an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (OU) model for the evolution of the average phenotype, our fitness formulation leads to a previously unrecognized family of stochastic differential equation models of trait evolution. These results provide mechanistic justification for non-Gaussian fitness functions (often observed in natural systems), provide a path for testing alternative models generating non-Gaussian fitness functions, and pave the way for future study of the interplay of ecological and evolutionary dynamics, such as in the study of evolutionary rescue.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Non-equivalence of pro-$p$-Iwahori invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19570)
*Karol Koziol*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明：当F是非阿基米德局部域（剩余域为F_p的真扩张，p>3）时，任何非环面的分裂连通约化群G在F上均具有光滑、不可约但非可容许的mod p表示，推广了Ghate-Le-Sheth的结论。并由此证明pro-p-Iwahori不变子函子不诱导G表示范畴与pro-p-Iwahori-Hecke代数模范畴之间的等价，与GL_2(Q_p)的情形形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非阿基米德局部域上的约化群在mod p表示理论中，光滑不可约表示与可容许性之间的关系，并探究pro-p-Iwahori-Hecke代数与表示范畴之间的等价性在更一般情形下是否成立。

Method: 推广Ghate-Le-Sheth的构造方法，针对剩余域为F_p真扩张的情形构造光滑、不可约、非可容许的mod p表示，并利用该反例分析pro-p-Iwahori不变子函子的性质。

Result: 任何非环面的分裂连通约化群G均存在光滑、不可约、非可容许的mod p表示；由此证明pro-p-Iwahori不变子函子不诱导表示范畴与Hecke代数模范畴间的等价。

Conclusion: pro-p-Iwahori-Hecke代数与表示范畴等价的良好性质仅在GL_2(Q_p)等特殊情形成立，在更一般的分裂连通约化群（剩余域为F_p真扩张）上该等价关系失效。

Abstract: Suppose $F$ is a nonarchimedean local field whose residue field is a proper extension of $\mathbb{F}_p$ with $p > 3$. Generalizing results of Ghate--Le--Sheth, we show that any split, connected, reductive group $G$ over $F$ which is not a torus admits a smooth, irreducible, non-admissible mod $p$ representation. We use this to show that the functor of pro-$p$-Iwahori invariants does not induce an equivalence between the category of smooth mod $p$ $G$-representations generated by their pro-$p$-Iwahori invariant vectors and modules over the pro-$p$-Iwahori--Hecke algebra, contrary to what happens for $\textrm{GL}_2(\mathbb{Q}_p)$.

</details>


### [113] [The subregular and submaximal $p$-cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19798)
*Vanessa Miemietz,Marie Roth,Daniel Tubbenhauer*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文确定了经典类型中Hecke范畴的子正则胞腔和子极大胞腔中的p-典范基元素及其p-胞腔结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典范基和p-典范基的胞腔（cells）组织着Hecke范畴的表示论与几何结构。确定p-典范基元素及其产生的p-胞腔结构对于理解这些范畴的模块表示和几何性质至关重要，特别是在所有经典类型中。

Method: 针对所有经典类型的Hecke范畴，系统地确定子正则（subregular）和子极大（submaximal）胞腔中相关的p-典范基元素，并由此推导出相应的p-胞腔结构。

Result: 在所有经典类型中，完整确定了子正则和子极大胞腔中的p-典范基元素，并给出了相应的p-胞腔结构。

Conclusion: 这项工作填补了经典类型中Hecke范畴p-典范基胞腔结构的关键空白，为后续研究这类范畴的表示论与几何性质奠定了基础。

Abstract: Cells for the canonical and $p$-canonical bases organise the representation theory and geometry of Hecke categories. We determine the relevant $p$-canonical basis elements and the resulting $p$-cell structure for the subregular and submaximal cells in all classical types.

</details>


### [114] [Categorification in Representation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19830)
*Vanessa Miemietz*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述文章，对相对年轻的2-表示理论领域进行了介绍，该理论研究经典表示理论中重要对象的范畴化。文章重点梳理了导致有限Weyl群在特征0下Soergel双模的简单2-表示分类的主要结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2-表示理论是一个相对较新的领域，涉及经典表示理论的范畴化，需要对这一领域进行系统性的介绍和梳理，以便读者理解其核心内容及最新进展。

Method: 综述方法，对流式地介绍了2-表示理论的基本概念，并系统梳理了从基本定义到简单2-表示分类的主要结果，特别关注Soergel双模在有限Weyl群（特征0）情形下的分类工作。

Result: 提供了对Soergel双模的简单2-表示分类定理的流线型（streamlined）阐述，该分类结果由Mackaay、Mazorchuk、Miemietz、Tubbenhauer和Zhang于2023年在Proc. London Math. Soc.上发表。

Conclusion: 本文作为综述，为读者提供了进入2-表示理论领域的导引，并清晰呈现了有限Weyl群情形下Soergel双模简单2-表示分类这一重要成果的完整逻辑链条。

Abstract: In this survey article, we give an introduction to the relatively young subject of $2$-representation theory, which studies categorifications of important players in classical representation theory. In particular, we provide a streamlined exposition of the main results leading to the classification of simple $2$-representations for Soergel bimodules associated to finite Weyl groups in characteristic $0$, obtained in [M. Mackaay, V. Mazorchuk, V. Miemietz, D. Tubbenhauer and X. Zhang, Simple transitive $2$-representations of Soergel bimodules for finite Coxeter types. Proc. London Math. Soc. 126 (2023)].

</details>


### [115] [Recollements of derived categories from $n$-term big tilting complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19921)
*Shengyong Pan,Huabo Xu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究环A上的n-项大倾斜复形（big tilting complex）所诱导的导出范畴recollement结构。通过构造扩张封闭的精确子范畴E，证明导出范畴D(B)由D(E)和D(A)实现recollement，并进一步研究了E的对称性、abelian性等性质，将经典两项情形的普遍局部化构造推广到任意有限项大倾斜复形，并给出每个n≥2的真n-项非紧致大倾斜复形的构造实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两项大倾斜复形（即倾斜模）已被深入研究，其对应的recollement结构由同调环满态射诱导，并且核是abelian的。本文旨在将这一经典理论推广到任意有限项（n项）的大倾斜复形，处理更高amplitude（振幅）情形下核不再是abelian范畴的问题，从而需要引入精确范畴作为替代。

Method: 作者通过dg双重中心化（dg double-centralizer）描述构造T的投射模型，并利用精确实现定理（exact realisation theorem）将D(E)等同于导出张量函子的核。在此基础上，构造扩张封闭的精确子范畴E，证明D(B)关于D(E)和D(A)的recollement，并分析E的d-对称性及abelian性的充要条件。最后通过代数Calkin型商给出明确的三项实例。

Result: 证明了D(B)由D(E)和D(A)给出recollement，其中E是B-模范畴的扩张封闭精确子范畴；E是d-对称的（d不小于T的右B-模型振幅）；E为abelian当且仅当核在标准t-结构下稳定，等价于recollement由同调环满态射诱导；在右B-振幅≤1时核为abelian，恢复两项情形的普遍局部化recollement；对每个n≥2构造了真n-项非紧致大倾斜复形，包括三项显式例子。

Conclusion: 经典两项大倾斜复形的recollement理论被完整推广到任意有限项情形，精确范畴自然地取代更高振幅下abelian核的角色。该工作不仅给出了一般框架，还提供了明确的反例和构造，说明在更高振幅下abelian性确实失效，从而精确范畴的引入是本质的而非技术性的。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a ring and let $\bf T$ be an $n$-term big tilting complex over $A$, represented by a bounded complex $\cpx{P}$ of projective $A$-modules. Set $B=\End_{\D{A}}(\cpx{P}), Λ:=\dotEnd_A(\cpx{P})$ and $Δ:=τ_{\leq 0}Λ$. The associated complex of $A$-$B$-bimodule $\cpx{T}=\cpx{P}\otimesL_ΔB$ is given. We construct an extension-closed exact subcategory $\mathscr E$ of $B\Modcat$ and prove that the derived category $\D{B}$ admits a recollement by $\D{\mathscr E}$ and $\D{A}$. The proof passes through a dg double-centralizer description of a projective model of $\cpx{T}$ and an exact realisation theorem identifying $\D{\mathscr E}$ with the kernel of the derived tensor functor. We further show that $\mathscr E$ is $d$-symmetric for every $d$ not smaller than the amplitude of a perfect right $B$-model of $\cpx{T}$. Moreover, $\mathscr E$ is abelian if and only if the kernel is stable under the standard $t$-structure, equivalently, the recollement is induced by a homological ring epimorphism. In right $B$-amplitude at most one, the kernel is therefore abelian, and our construction specializes to the recollement arising from universal localisation in the two-term case. Thus the classical two-term picture extends to arbitrary finite-term big tilting complexes, with exact categories replacing abelian kernels in higher amplitude. Finally, we construct genuinely $n$-term non-compact big tilting complexes for every $n\geq2$, including an explicit three-term example whose left-hand term is determined by an algebraic Calkin-type quotient.

</details>


### [116] [The Local Langlands Correspondence for Middle Supercuspidal Representations of $p$-adic $\text{GL}(2n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20225)
*David C. Luo,Shaun Stevens*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在非阿基米德局部域（特征为零，剩余特征为p）上，在条件 p∤2n 下，显式描述了 GL(2n,F) 上 middle supercuspidal 表示在局部 Langlands 对应中的目标对象，通过最大简单类型和伽马因子的显式计算来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部 Langlands 对应是数论中核心但深奥的对象，对一般超尖点表示的具体对应关系尚未得到显式描述。作者希望填补 middle supercuspidal 表示这一具体类别的显式描述空白，通过伽马因子这一关键不变量来刻画对应关系。

Method: 利用最大简单类型（maximal simple types）定义超尖点表示，并在 tame 条件 p∤2n 下，分别在自守侧和 Galois 侧显式计算并比较伽马因子，从而确定局部 Langlands 对应的具体对应关系。自守侧的伽马因子计算无需 tame 条件或特征为零的条件。

Result: 在 p∤2n 的 tame 条件下，得到了 GL(2n,F) 上 middle supercuspidal 表示在局部 Langlands 对应下的显式描述，并以定义它们的最大简单类型来刻画其对应。

Conclusion: 通过伽马因子的双侧显式计算，建立了 middle supercuspidal 表示与 Galois 侧对象之间的精确对应，且自守侧的伽马因子计算具有更广泛的适用范围（不依赖 tame 和特征零假设）。

Abstract: Let $\text{F}$ be a non-archimedean local field of characteristic zero with residual characteristic $p$. In this paper we give an explicit description of the local Langlands correspondence for middle supercuspidal representations of $\text{GL}(2n,\text{F})$, under the tameness condition $p\nmid 2n$, in terms of the maximal simple types that define them. We achieve this by explicitly computing and comparing the gamma factors on the automorphic and Galois sides of the local Langlands correspondence. The computation on the automorphic side requires neither the tameness condition nor the characteristic zero condition.

</details>


### [117] [Complete Symbols of Equivariant Pseudodifferential Operators on Noncompact Symmetric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20313)
*Satwata Hans*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了非紧对称空间 G/K 上的 G-等变 Hörmander 伪微分算子，定义了在球面温和对偶上的完整符号函数（Harish-Chandra 符号函数），并利用该符号函数刻画了 Schwartz 核具有快速非对角线衰减性质的算子类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非紧对称空间上的等变伪微分算子，需要一种自然且完整的符号理论来刻画具有良好核衰减性质的算子类。现有的Hörmander伪微分算子理论在对称空间等变框架下的符号化描述尚未完善，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在非紧对称空间G/K上，引入G-等变Hörmander伪微分算子，通过在球面温和对偶上定义Harish-Chandra符号函数，给出满足Schwartz核快速非对角线衰减条件的算子的完整符号刻画。

Result: 定义了Harish-Chandra符号函数的概念，并证明了一类具有快速非对角线核衰减的G-等变伪微分算子可以完全由其Harish-Chandra符号函数来表征。

Conclusion: 在非紧对称空间的等变框架下，建立了基于球面温和对偶的完整符号理论，为后续研究该类算子的性质（如有界性、谱分析等）提供了基础工具。

Abstract: We study $G$-equivariant Hörmander pseudodifferential operators on a noncompact symmetric space $G/K$. We define a notion of a complete symbol function, called the Harish-Chandra symbol function, on the spherical tempered dual of $G$, for operators that satisfy a rapid off-diagonal decay condition on their Schwartz kernels, and we characterize the class of such operators in terms of their Harish-Chandra symbol function.

</details>

### [118] [Moore--Read construction and explicit monodromies of Laughlin states on Riemann surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19619)
*Kiyoon Eum*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过CS/WZW对应回顾了紧致黎曼曲面上ν=1/k Laughlin态的Moore-Read构造，从U(1)_k Chern-Simons理论推导其共形块，计算准空穴输运与Aharonov-Bohm磁通插入下的显式单值性，并猜想其与绝热和乐一致，从而恢复Laughlin态经典结果（如通量平均霍尔电导1/k）并联系到高亏格Laughlin态的代数几何方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在紧致黎曼曲面（高亏格）上系统化地构造和验证Laughlin分数量子霍尔态，建立其共形块、单值性与拓扑量子场论之间的联系，并统一不同数学框架（Chern-Simons/WZW与代数几何方法）。

Method: 利用CS/WZW对应，从U(1)_k Chern-Simons理论出发在紧致黎曼曲面上推导Laughlin态共形块；通过准空穴输运和AB磁通插入显式计算其单值性；在Jacobi簇上构造Laughlin态向量丛以恢复霍尔电导等经典结果；并与已有的高亏格Laughlin态代数几何方法建立联系。

Result: 获得了Laughlin态在准空穴输运和AB磁通插入下的显式单值性表示，提出了其与绝热和乐一致的猜想；在此识别下恢复了通量平均霍尔电导1/k；建立了与高亏格Laughlin态代数几何方法的关联。

Conclusion: 该工作为高亏格情形下Laughlin态的拓扑性质提供了统一的Chern-Simons理论框架，确立了共形块单值性与物理绝热和乐的等价性猜想，并架起了与代数几何方法之间的桥梁。

Abstract: We revisit the Moore--Read construction of the $ν=1/k$ Laughlin states on compact Riemann surfaces, deriving their conformal blocks from $U(1)_k$ Chern--Simons theory through the CS/WZW correspondence. We compute their explicit monodromies under quasi-hole transport and Aharonov--Bohm flux insertion, and conjecture that these coincide with the corresponding adiabatic holonomies. Under this identification, we recover classical results on Laughlin states including the flux-averaged Hall conductance $1/k$ via a vector bundle of Laughlin states over $Jac(Σ)$. We also relate our construction to the recently developed algebro-geometric approach to higher genus Laughlin states.

</details>
